# hipEngine Benchmark Changelog

Reverse-chronological human-readable history for benchmark rollup changes. Keep
entries short; detailed evidence belongs in `benchmarks/results/*.json` and
`WORKLOG.md`.

Entry format:

```text
- [scope] model / quant / workload: metric old -> new (+/-X%) due to reason/change; artifact/source.
```

Examples:

```text
- [hipEngine] Qwen3-0.6B / fp16 / 4K/4K: decode 80.0 -> 88.0 tok/s (+10.0%) due to paged-attn split-K; `benchmarks/results/2026-..json`.
- [lineage target] Qwen3.5-PARO / w4a16 / 512/128: prefill 1300 -> 2557 tok/s (+96.7%) due to compact WMMA; `~/amd-gpu-tuning/docs/OPTIMAL.md`.
```

## 2026-07-26

- [runtime-correctness-admitted default-off gfx1100 Laguna Q2 XL exact SWA local64 dim2 reducer] Radeon Pro W7900 Poolside Laguna S 2.1 UD-Q2_K_XL / retained local128 one-head wave-local reducer -> explicit local64 packed-dim2 selector preserves full logits/IDs, all 48 hidden/47 routed boundaries, active K/V and every span field through 16 transitions; cached tracing records 36 local64/grid4608/VGPR24/LDS0/scratch0 calls/token, zero retained/shared reducer calls, and unchanged 723 model kernels/token. Clean-context/category gates remain pending, so canonical h32 stays **61.732 tok/s**; `benchmarks/results/2026-07-26-gfx1100-laguna-q2-xl-swa-local64-dim2-runtime-correctness.json`.

- [primitive-admitted runtime-unselected gfx1100 Laguna Q2 XL exact SWA local64 dim2 reducer] Radeon Pro W7900 Poolside Laguna S 2.1 UD-Q2_K_XL / retained local128 one-head wave-local reducer -> one local64 block still owns each of 72 query heads while packed adjacent-dimension loads halve replicated scalar/load instructions; F32/BF16 outputs are bit-exact, cached resources are local64/VGPR24/LDS0/scratch0, and all layers-1/46/47 live-70/128/257/512 actual rows improve HIP event **0.275-0.685%** and synchronized wall **0.295-2.294%**. Runtime/full-state/context/category gates remain pending, so canonical h32 stays **61.732 tok/s**; `benchmarks/results/2026-07-26-gfx1100-laguna-q2-xl-swa-local64-dim2-reducer-correctness.json`.

- [post-local32 matched Laguna completion audit, Vulkan still faster] Radeon Pro W7900 Poolside Laguna S 2.1 UD-Q2_K_XL / all 18 natural-greedy category+heldout prompts at context 4096 and transition-normalized h16/h32: retained all-local32 hipEngine **62.354/61.732 tok/s** versus device-pinned llama.cpp Vulkan **64.245/64.418 tok/s** (**-2.94%/-4.17%**), requiring **+3.03%/+4.35%** more hipEngine throughput; exact current two-order category/state evidence is composed with the unchanged valid 72-row pinned Vulkan measurement, so completion still fails; `benchmarks/results/2026-07-26-gfx1100-laguna-q2-xl-vulkan-matched-completion-post-local32.json`.

- [retained gfx1100 Laguna Q2 XL exact all-local32 mixed projections] Radeon Pro W7900 Poolside Laguna S 2.1 UD-Q2_K_XL / retained local128 fixed-Q6 Q5/Q6 quad -> exact independent local32 Q5/Q6 output-pair owners move two-order 18-prompt h32 decode **60.900 -> 61.732 tok/s (+1.367%)**, or prior retained **60.942 -> 61.732 (+1.296%)**; every train/heldout category decode improves, clean projection/kernel/span/child rows improve **7.00-8.12% / 0.49-2.12% / 0.45-2.77% / 0.20-1.29%**, full state remains exact, and dispatches stay 723/token. Pinned Vulkan is still faster at **64.418 tok/s**, requiring another **4.35%**; `benchmarks/results/2026-07-26-gfx1100-laguna-q2-xl-mixed-local32-projection-retained.json`.

- [micro correctness attribution] Radeon 8060S/gfx1151 Vulkan Q4/Q6 strict fixture boundaries: shared Vulkan-q8 input -> **CPU-prequantized q8 isolation**, changing Q4 KL `0.079520 -> 4.46e-51` with top-1 `1.0`, and Q6 top-1 `0.875 -> 1.0` with KL `0.007756 -> 3.20e-12`. Readback finds every mismatched `d` exactly one FP16 code low (`1,374/2,816` Q4 blocks; `1,686/2,304` Q6), while a temporary RNE-pack control clears both end-to-end gates. This attributes the misses to a portable q8_1 rounding contract, not dot math, synchronization, or a Mesa `.2 -> .4` regression; no performance row changes. Boundary wall `2.014s` Q4 / `0.877s` Q6; `benchmarks/results/2026-07-12-gfx1151-vulkan-q8-isolation-diagnostic.json`.

- [micro layout retained] gfx1151 / Radeon 8060S / HIP q8_1 real-slice layout controls: Q4_K selected-dual gate/up q8_1 quantize+dp4a measured **0.3458 ms** versus raw selected-dual **0.9584 ms** (**2.77x**), q8_1 quantize alone **0.00247 ms**, KL mean **0.00311**, top-1 **1.0**; Q6_K selected-down X8 q8_1 quantize+dp4a measured **0.01925 ms** versus production T16 float **0.03228 ms** (**1.68x**), q8_1 quantize alone **0.00275 ms**, KL mean **0.00137**, top-1 **1.0**. Classified `layout_quant`; HIP-only evidence that q8_1 materialization is not the blocker, not a matched Vulkan real-slice result. `benchmarks/micro/results/gfx1151/strix-halo/hip-real-q4-selected-dual-q8_1-dp4a.json`, `benchmarks/micro/results/gfx1151/strix-halo/hip-real-q6-selected-down-x8-q8_1-dp4a.json`.
- [micro diagnostic retained] gfx1151 / Radeon 8060S / HIP fixed-shape controls for dot-path, memory/waitcnt, and f32 geometry: fixed block/workgroup specialization passed correctness but did not close the Vulkan gap. Dot fixed-block indexing was **0.993x-1.000x** versus same-commit runtime HIP and still trailed Vulkan by **3.31x-3.43x**; memory fixed-block indexing was mixed (**0.906x-1.290x** fixed/runtime) and Vulkan remained faster on every row (**1.04x-2.36x**); fixed-workgroup geometry improved some HIP wg256 rows by up to **6.3%**, but Vulkan still led best-native geometry by **5.56x-14.03x**. Classified `diagnostic_unclassified`; runtime `blockDim`/specialization is not the missing switch. `benchmarks/micro/results/gfx1151/strix-halo/dot-path-fixed-block-comparison.json`, `benchmarks/micro/results/gfx1151/strix-halo/memory-waitcnt-fixed-block-comparison.json`, `benchmarks/micro/results/gfx1151/strix-halo/geometry-sweep-fixed-workgroup-comparison.json`.
- [micro diagnostic retained] gfx1151 / Radeon 8060S / HIP wave64 controls for dot-path and memory/waitcnt: forcing HIP `-mwavefrontsize64` passed correctness and reported wave64/no scratch, but did not close the Vulkan gap. Dot-path wave64 was **1.007x-1.061x slower** than HIP wave32 and still trailed same-commit Vulkan by **2.63x-3.55x**; memory/waitcnt wave64 was mixed, still left Vulkan faster on most rows, and regressed gather **6.349x** versus HIP wave32. Classified `diagnostic_unclassified`; do not promote broad HIP wave64 routing. `benchmarks/micro/results/gfx1151/strix-halo/dot-path-wave64-comparison.json`, `benchmarks/micro/results/gfx1151/strix-halo/memory-waitcnt-wave64-comparison.json`.
- [micro diagnostic retained] gfx1151 / Radeon 8060S / HIP vs Vulkan packed dot-path sweep: q8 signed, q4 unsigned-byte by signed-q8, q6 zero-corrected, and scalar q4 rows all passed exact sampled CPU oracle. HIP and RADV both emitted final dot4 instructions in q8/q4/q6 rows, HIP reported **0 scratch/spills**, and Vulkan was **3.29x-3.43x** faster including scalar dequant. Classified `diagnostic_unclassified`; rules out missed HIP dot4, but not wave/fixed-shape/surrounding scheduling confounds. `benchmarks/micro/results/gfx1151/strix-halo/dot-path-comparison.json`.
- [micro diagnostic retained] gfx1151 / Radeon 8060S / HIP vs Vulkan memory/waitcnt sweep: device-memory coalesced, strided, gather, and load/compute interleave rows all passed sampled CPU oracle. Vulkan was **1.30x-2.35x** faster on most coalesced/strided/interleave rows, with gather essentially tied (**1.02x**). HIP reported wave32 and **0 scratch/spills**; RADV final shaders were wave64 with only estimated register spans. Classified `diagnostic_unclassified`; strong memory-side evidence, but not a clean `compiler_aco` proof until wave/specialization controls land. `benchmarks/micro/results/gfx1151/strix-halo/memory-waitcnt-comparison.json`.
- [micro diagnostic retained] gfx1151 / Radeon 8060S / HIP vs Vulkan VOPD scheduling sweep: pure VALU variants all passed sampled CPU oracle; HIP emitted VOPD in every retained row (`4/9/12` VOPD instructions for independent f32 FMA 2/4/8 accumulators) while RADV final disassembly emitted **0 VOPD** in every row. Vulkan was modestly faster only on independent-8 (**1.05x**), mixed int+float (**1.08x**), and dequant-like (**1.04x**), while HIP was faster on independent-2/4 and dependent-4. Classified `diagnostic_unclassified`; negative result for "ACO wins through VOPD." `benchmarks/micro/results/gfx1151/strix-halo/vopd-sweep-comparison.json`.
- [micro diagnostic retained] gfx1151 / Radeon 8060S / HIP vs Vulkan f32 geometry ISA/stat extraction: K=`2048`, rows=`1`, wg=`64/256` extraction shows HIP actual code-object metadata at **18 SGPR / 11 VGPR**, **0 scratch/spills**, wave32, and **2 VOPD** instructions, while RADV final disassembly has wave64 and **0 VOPD** with no official RADV allocation counts exposed. This rules out a simple missed-HIP-VOPD or HIP-spill explanation for the retained geometry timing gap but remains `diagnostic_unclassified`. `benchmarks/micro/results/gfx1151/strix-halo/geometry-isa-stats-comparison.json`.
- [micro diagnostic retained] gfx1151 / Radeon 8060S / HIP vs Vulkan f32 GEMV geometry sweep: repeat-shifted matched K=`512/2048/8192`, rows=`1/4/8`, workgroup=`32/64/128/256` rows all passed CPU oracle; HIP and Vulkan both prefer wg256, so workgroup shape alone does not explain the gap, while Vulkan remains **5.79x-14.03x** faster on best-native rows. Classified `diagnostic_unclassified`; paired ISA extraction now rules out a simple missed-HIP-VOPD explanation but does not identify the primary cause. `benchmarks/micro/results/gfx1151/strix-halo/geometry-sweep-comparison.json`.
- [micro diagnostic retained] gfx1151 / Radeon 8060S / HIP vs Vulkan dispatch-grid floor: Vulkan command-buffer replay measured **0.043621 us/dispatch** at N=941 one-block versus HIP tiny direct **2.0087 us** and HIP graph **1.8069 us** (**46.0x / 41.4x** runtime-dispatch gap); at 8192 blocks the gap narrowed to Vulkan **11.9779 us** versus HIP wide direct **13.2324 us** and HIP graph **13.0226 us** (**1.10x / 1.09x**). Classified `runtime_dispatch`, not `compiler_aco`; this is not shader-codegen evidence. `benchmarks/micro/results/gfx1151/strix-halo/dispatch-floor-comparison.json`.

## 2026-06-09

- [diagnostic] hipEngine / MTP economics C_B A/B (close T1) / W7900 gfx1100: **GDN dv-tiling does NOT move C_B; T1 safe on real prompts.** Same-prompt A/B (quicksort 90-tok, decode 32, B=3, 3 runs each), strict shuffle vs relaxed dv-tiled: **C_B 4.81 ± 0.14 -> 4.80 ± 0.28 (unchanged within noise)**, acceptance byte-identical (0.4615, std 0), `all_exact_ar_match=true` for ALL 6 runs. The `exact_ar_match` flip is degenerate-1-token-only; on the real prompt the ~1 ULP changes nothing. The -0.56 ms/pass kernel saving is below the cycle-wall noise (~0.6-1.2 ms) because the verify cycle is dispatch/host-bound (~19.4 ms floor), not kernel-bound -- reinforces MEGAKERNEL §9.2. dv-tiling = banked kernel-time headroom. First landed relaxed-mode profile fully characterized in docs/RELAXED.md §0.1 (FP reassociation, ~1-2 ULP, drift-tier T2, strict VTILE=1 fallback). `benchmarks/results/2026-06-09-hipengine-m16-gdn-dvtiling-economics-cb.json`.
- [hipEngine] Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-PARO packed MTP-BF16 / MTP verifier W7900 gfx1100 B=3: **M16.6 GDN chain dv-tiling (first win under T1)** — `qwen35_gdn_chain_recurrent` 72.0 -> 53.39 µs/call (-25.8%; -32% vs original 78.5), 2.16 -> 1.602 ms/pass (-0.56 ms/pass), grid **4096 -> 1024 blocks** (VGPR 64, Scratch 0). dv-tiling: each block owns VTILE=4 consecutive dv columns, so per-(v_head,t) q/k load + q/k-scale reductions + beta/decay transcendentals are computed once/tile and the 4 dv state writes coalesce. Microbench vs cpu_reference T4 86.99 -> 67.76 µs (-22%), T8 155.02 -> 118.42 µs (-24%); out_max_abs 1.07e-6, leaf 5.96e-8 (**KL << 0.05**); 555 GDN/linear_attn tests pass; tree DFlash kernel untouched. Landed under **T1** (verify-path gate = KL/top-1 vs cpu_reference, adopted 2026-06-09): `exact_ar_match` flips true->false via ~1 ULP FP-reorder at the degenerate 1-token-prompt boundary — accepted, not a KL regression (see docs/MEGAKERNEL.md §5/§8.1, docs/TESTING.md §4). Owed: AR tok/s + acceptance-rate re-baseline on real prompts before any MTP economics claim. `benchmarks/results/2026-06-09-hipengine-m16-gdn-chain-dvtiling.json`.
- [diagnostic] hipEngine / dispatch model re-test / W7900 gfx1100: **M16 grid-sweep retest** confirms per-launch dispatch = ~5.6 µs base + grid term (1024=7.25, 2048=7.95, 4096=9.36, 8192=12.34 µs/launch), graph-neutral (1.00-1.15x), arg-independent (2->16 args +0.0 µs). Re-confirms M16.2; grounds the grid-reduction lever (rocprof kernel-trace cannot see this floor — ROOFLINE §5.3). `benchmarks/results/2026-06-09-hipengine-m16-dispatch-grid-sweep-retest.json`.
- [hipEngine] Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-PARO packed MTP-BF16 / MTP verifier W7900 gfx1100 B=3: **M16.5 GDN chain shuffle reductions** — `qwen35_gdn_chain_recurrent` 78.5 -> 72.0 µs/call (-8.3%), GDN family 14.137 -> 12.959 ms/180 calls (2.36 -> 2.16 ms/pass), total verifier kernel 13.84 -> 13.61 ms/pass, due to replacing the 4 per-token LDS+`__syncthreads` block tree-reductions with single-wavefront warp-shuffle reductions (32-thread block when head_k_dim<=128). Exact AR (`exact_ar_match=true`); microbench vs numpy delta-rule oracle out_max_abs 9.5e-7, state 6.0e-8; 30 GDN/linear_attn tests pass; tree DFlash kernel untouched. `benchmarks/results/2026-06-09-hipengine-mtp-m16.5-gdn-chain-shuffle-reductions.json`.
- [diagnostic] hipEngine / Qwen3.6-35B-A3B GGUF Q4_K / decode-shaped selected MoE FFN / W7900 gfx1100: **B5 batched-occupancy sweep — occupancy hypothesis CONFIRMED.** Fused MoE FFN megakernel vs unfused raw chain across c (concurrent tokens), rows=c*top_k: speedup rises `1.55x (c=1) -> 4.47x (c=8, 64 blocks)` then falls (`3.35x c=16`, `1.88x c=32`); KL~0 throughout; launches/layer 3->1. Batching lifts the megakernel out of the single-token occupancy wall exactly as predicted. **But no GGUF concurrent c>1 decode E2E path exists** (c=1-only harness; rows>1 = prefill where WMMA beats the fused GEMV). The batched-small-rows win regime IS the speculative verify shape → deployment target is the verify/C_B regime (PARO), not GGUF decode. Completes the GGUF megakernel arc B0-B5. `benchmarks/results/2026-06-09-hipengine-gguf-q4ks-b5-fused-moe-ffn-batched-occupancy.json`.
- [diagnostic not-promoted] hipEngine / Qwen3.6-35B-A3B GGUF Q4_K_S / 512/128 decode / W7900 gfx1100: **B2 fused MoE FFN megakernel wired (gated off)** — `HIPENGINE_GGUF_FUSED_MOE_FFN` routes the rows==1 selected-expert FFN through the B1 fused kernel (gate_up->silu->down in one launch, 3->1 launches/layer). Raw-Q4_K decode 9.859 -> 11.343 tok/s (+15.1%); microbench FFN 1.58x vs unfused raw chain (0.420 -> 0.266 ms/call). **Does NOT beat the deployed T16 baseline (92.936 tok/s)** because the raw megakernel does not apply to the T16 decode-repack path; kept gated off. Kernel correct: oracle-exact on real layer-0 weights (max_rel 1.2e-8), and fused-vs-unfused `moe_down_out` matches in situ to ~1 bf16 ULP (max_abs 1.22e-4); passes KL<=0.05 vs cpu_reference. The whole-model teacher-forced KL is large (1.09/32 tok) purely as 40-layer + KV-drift accumulation of the unavoidable ~1-ULP bf16 reduction-order difference (any kernel swap shifts the exact E2E token stream). Best megakernel target is batched (c>1) GGUF decode (c*8 blocks lifts the single-token occupancy wall) and the verify/C_B regime. `benchmarks/results/2026-06-09-hipengine-gguf-q4ks-b2-fused-moe-ffn-decode-diagnostic.json`.

- [diagnostic retained] hipEngine / M16.2 launch-cost arg+grid scaling / W7900 gfx1100: per-launch host cost is arg-count-independent (2-arg 5.62 = 16-arg 5.62 us/launch at N=941) but SCALES with grid size (1024 blk 7.27, 8192 12.19, 65536 51.40 us/launch); graph replay only 1.02-1.13x even at large grids. Real hot W4 GEMVs launch thousands of blocks, so the ~12-20 us/op residual is GPU workgroup scheduling, not host/Python (M14.dispatch.1 parity) nor arg-marshaling. DISPROVES M16.2's premise: a native verify loop is predicted parity; graphs (M16.5) neutral; the sole launch-residual lever is M16.3 (fewer/larger kernels). Corrects the M16.1 'native loop is the lever' read. `benchmarks/results/2026-06-08-hipengine-m16.2-launch-cost-arg-grid-scaling.json`.

M7 phase reach revised to ~1 ms kernel + ~4 ms wall (prefill kernels at 4 tokens were less wasteful than the M7.C projection assumed); `benchmarks/results/2026-05-21-hipengine-mtp-m7c6-small-batch-dispatch-split.json`.
